### E219 — Archived-weather cutoff audit and pre-weekend guard repair

**Previous goal turn:** the requested social-media research prompt was delivered; no experiment or model result changed. No external research agent is verified live. Continue the active 75% exact-winner objective using authoritative repository state.

**Question and alternatives:** either existing rolling three-day forecast fields satisfy the fixed Thursday 00:00 UTC cutoff, or an explicitly timestamped older model run is required. Official Open-Meteo Previous Runs documentation states that offsets are relative to each valid hour, not a common publication time; most models start January 2024 (GFS temperature March 2021). Single Runs documentation starts ECMWF March 2024 and other models April 2026. Neither supplies a certified common-issue full 2020–22 source. A Sunday daytime valid time minus 72 hours can fall after Thursday 00:00. Initialization is not publication. Existing fc3 fields are therefore uncertified, not valid pre-weekend inputs by default. Source docs: https://open-meteo.com/en/docs/previous-runs-api and https://open-meteo.com/en/docs/single-runs-api (read 2026-11-18).

**Verified bug:** assert_pre_weekend accepted rain_act/temp_act/wind_act and uncertified fc3_rain/fc3_temp. Frozen pre_weekend_cols does not select these; no new evidence of leakage in frozen scores. Added a regression test first: it failed with `guard did not block rain_act`. Shared exact-name guard now rejects those fields plus legacy E16 target-weather transformations wetrace/rain_x_skill/rain_x_form/rain_x_gridless. Historical d_wet_skill and d3_temp_act remain accepted. Corrected weather.py's misleading legality docstring; no data acquisition, forecast refresh, feature calculation, model scoring, or production selection changed.

**Contract:** feature names supplied to the pre-weekend guard containing these known uncertified/actual target-weather names raise ValueError, including before walk_forward accesses data/models. Empty/valid historical feature lists remain legal; post-qualifying mode is unaffected. Exact-name checks deliberately do not outlaw all future certified forecasts. Limitation: a name blacklist is not provenance certification and cannot detect arbitrary renamed fields or models hiding additional inputs in closures (legacy E16 oracle code does the latter and is explicitly oracle-labelled). No claim of universal leakage prevention.

**Verification:** direct guard and actual walk_forward boundary reject every one of nine weather fields; empty data/missing model ensure rejection precedes evaluation. All five required leak/preweekend/profile/guard/metrics suites pass; their standard 2026 mutation checks are not outcome evaluations. Adversarial review considered historical-name false positives and hidden closure bypass. Inner/outer accuracy unchanged; no new paired winner bootstrap/null applicable because no predictions changed. E16's historical podium-oracle negative does not prove all forecast weather signals useless for the revised winner objective.

**Next source avenue:** public NOAA GEFS archive, metadata-only access/publication audit before any forecast value extraction. NOAA public registry permits anonymous access without an account or interactive licence acceptance: https://registry.opendata.aws/noaa-gefs/. No paid/account/market/document-text model input. 75% exact all-race pre-weekend winner goal remains active/unmet; strict podium overlap secondary.

### E220 — Public GEFS historical availability metadata pilot (registered before requests)

**Purpose:** find whether an anonymous structured forecast archive has source-time evidence covering early and later development eras. This is a source-access study, not a winner experiment. Alternatives: historical file modification receipts predate the cutoff; alternatively objects are absent/inaccessible/backfilled and this particular metadata is insufficient. Neither absence nor later modification proves the original forecast was unavailable elsewhere.

**Frozen scope:** exactly nine unauthenticated HEAD requests, no retry/redirect/body/GRIB decoding/listing/new endpoints. Host noaa-gefs-pds.s3.amazonaws.com; Spain race-week Wednesday 12Z cycles 20190508,20200812,20220518. Thursday cutoff is next day 00Z (12-hour buffer is an assumption, not proof of publication). For each day try all three fixed names under gefs.YYYYMMDD/12/: pgrb2a/gec00.t12z.pgrb2af96, pgrb2a/gec00.t12z.pgrb2af096, atmos/pgrb2ap5/gec00.t12z.pgrb2a.0p50.f096. Modern name is a candidate, not yet verified source format. Lead 96h points to Sunday12Z, but filename/initialization do not establish content or publication. Save HTTP status, length, Last-Modified, ETag, access time, cutoff, errors. Twenty-second timeout each. No credentials, accounts, payment, terms acceptance, race outcomes or weather values.

**Evidence gate:** each of three dates must have ≥1 HTTP200 positive-length object whose timezone-aware Last-Modified is ≤cutoff. This only permits a subsequent registered GRIB/reference-time/content-provenance audit, not feature/model use or a retrospective availability guarantee. Later Last-Modified is uncertified (possibly backfill); transient errors are inconclusive access observations. No date/lead/name substitutions after observing results. No inner/outer performance scored; no winner bootstrap/null yet applicable.

**Contract/checks:** before_cutoff returns false for absent/malformed/naive/late receipts, non200 status or nonpositive length; inclusive cutoff boundary passes. Pure tests exercise these without network. Runner requests only registered public HEAD URLs and saves receipts. Official current Requests HEAD/redirect/timeout docs read; NOAA NCEI documents NODD coverage2017+ but not official archival, and public AWS registry permits anonymous public access. Sources: https://requests.readthedocs.io/en/latest/user/quickstart/ ; https://www.ncei.noaa.gov/products/weather-climate-models/global-ensemble-forecast ; https://registry.opendata.aws/noaa-gefs/ ; https://github.com/awslabs/open-data-docs/tree/main/docs/noaa/noaa-gefs-pds . Commit/push setup before requests and results separately. No model selection or75% claim.

**Result (E220): metadata access gate passes all3/3 dates.** Setup2a1ef0d was committed/pushed before nine registered HEADs. Three HTTP200 objects, six HTTP404 alternatives, no request errors/redirects. Legacy f96 name exists for2019/2020, modern0p50.f096 for2022. Sizes4,291,706 /4,217,474 /14,795,578 bytes respectively (metadata only, none downloaded). Last-Modified2019-05-08 16:57:57Z /2020-08-12 16:48:46Z /2022-05-18 16:19:24Z. All precede Thursday00Z by7.0342 /7.1872 /7.6767hours; initialization-to-object lag4.9658 /4.8128 /4.3233hours. This supports pre-cutoff source availability for these three objects; it is not full historical coverage, a publication guarantee, file-content/reference-time verification, calibrated rainfall forecast skill, or a winner gain. File-size difference across eras is not decoded resolution evidence.

**Verification:** pure boundary tests pass. Independent mocked runner audit enforces exactly nine unique registered URLs, HEAD with timeout20 and redirectsFalse, allthree exact cutoffs, no admission/promotion. Independent saved-artifact reconstruction parses Last-Modified with strptime (rather than evaluator's email parser), verifies allcounts/init<modification<cutoff, sizes, datecoverage andflags. No GRIBbody/forecastvalue/observedweather acquired or2026outcome scored. Previous five standard guards passed inE219; E220 adds nofeature/model path. No paired winnerbootstrap/null applicable to metadata. Next permissible step: separately register bounded header/reference-time and field-inventory audit on these exact three objects, before any fullhistorical weather feature. Goal active/unmet; exactwinner andstrictoverlap unchanged.

### E231 — DriverScore indexed structure and publication audit: FAIL

Setup d20bad5 was pushed before exactly three authorized same-URL DriverScore re-GETs for 2019 Spain R5, Hungary R12 and Italy R14. All returned HTTP200 and raw SHA256 matched E230 exactly. Indexed numeric-only projections preserve list positions, dictionary indices and nulls; no free-form text or raw authenticated bodies retained. This was a source qualification experiment, not a winner model.

The frozen hypothesis that the outer score index represents laps fails: component-zero/actual-lap matches are 3/108 (2.78%), 3/120 (2.50%) and 5/120 (4.17%), versus the registered 95% requirement. Zero drivers qualify in each event. Records are 389/387/309, unique numeric coordinates 6864/8148/5952 and revised coordinates 4529/5824/4216. No unknown root car IDs or malformed records. Index dimensions alone do not establish channel meanings. The saved delay diagnostics depend on the failed lap interpretation and must not be treated as per-lap latency evidence. Do not transpose axes or select another component to rescue this registered gate.

Last-Modified headers are 2024-06-16 12:12:23, 16:11:02 and 17:13:05 GMT, all later than the registered historical publication limits of 2019-05-16, 2019-08-08 and 2019-09-12. Publication admission fails for all three. These later archive modification dates do not prove the data was originally unavailable in 2019; these receipts simply do not establish availability by the historical cutoff. No ad hoc availability assumption or new source request used to rescue admission.

Joint structure/roster/hash/publication gate FAILS 0/3, required at least 2/3. Independently reconstructed all 69,413 numeric values and paths against E230's projection, indexed observations, last-coordinate revisions, axis counts, component ranges, coverage, diagnostic delays and publication limits. Artifact audit and synthetic sparse-patch/index/text/empty/boundary tests pass. This verifies projection consistency and the failed gate, not physical semantics or an independent raw-body decoder. No production features changed; standard five guards last passed at E227 restoration. Saved results/e231_structure_summary.json and three e231_*_indexed.json.gz, with tests/test_driver_score_structure{,_artifact}.py.

No winner fit, inner/outer accuracy comparison, bootstrap/null or 2026 look applies to this source-only audit. Winner accuracy unchanged; best exploratory outer remains E179 34/70 (48.6%) versus points/ens 32/70 (45.7%), superiority unestablished. Goal 75% exact pre-weekend winner remains active/unmet, strict podium overlap secondary. Further work needs genuinely independent semantic and historical-provenance evidence, not relaxation of E231's failed axis or publication gates.

### E232 — Many-lap low-speed powered acceleration transit (registered before measurement)

**Previous turn:** progress: E231's independently verified failed structure/publication gates committed and pushed bef7f84. Goal remains 75% exact pre-weekend winners; no model promotion. Deliberate-thinking compares new acceleration measurement against further existing-source model tuning; choose measurement because no useful incremental winner evidence exists for another tuning sweep. Load-bearing assumption: repeated low-speed powered acceleration carries a portable car-response signal beyond pace form; competing explanation: track, gearing, tyres, fuel, traffic and driver choices dominate. Frozen future-signature and pace-error gates distinguish these; neither physical causation nor traction skill is assumed verified.

**Prior audit / novelty:** E9 measured distance to full throttle and speed gained over150m from fastest qualifying laps, plus 200–280km/h power proxies; E83–85 repeated a few qualifying laps, E97 high-speed deployment, E127–143 race spatial speed profiles, E211/E212 brake-on250→150transits and E213 powered→coast→brake sequences. Those formulations stay closed. No logged test uses repeated completed-race full-throttle100→150km/h transit durations across clean laps. This is related acceleration information, not an entirely unexplored physics family or a rescue of the old qualifying bands. No spatial curve derivative, energy/engine-power inference, wheelspin measurement or unknown DRS state.

**Fixed source and segment:** all21already cached2019Racecar feeds only; offline with HTTPAdapter.send blocked, no auth/acquisition/accounts/docs-as-input/outer/2026source. Reuse E190clean-lap selection and E211car-origin/lap boundaries unchanged: Date nonmissing, deduplicate last/sort, common origin max(Date−observedTime).round(ms), raw originalTime observed. Candidate crosses≤100 to>100km/h; continue consecutive finite nondecreasing speed samples with observedTime, Brake=False and98≤Throttle≤100, positive Date gap≤0.5seconds. First sample reaching≥150ends candidate. Require≥4samples and interpolated crossing duration0.4..4seconds inclusive; both bracketing endpoints in one half-open clean lap interval. Reject missing/gappy/nonmonotone/insufficient/partial segments. No accepted transit is missing, not zero acceleration. Raw sampling/interpolation approximates a speed transit, not exact tyre grip. All constants frozen before real-source measurement.

**Aggregation and gates:** reuse E211team_table unchanged (lap median, equal lap mean per driver with≥10laps; actual-LapNumber-parity halves≥5laps; exactly two measured drivers equal-weight team; center only≥6measured teams; retain all210rows). Reuse E204evaluate and E191past_response/cluster_interval unchanged: strictly earlier team history,10event half-life,5zero-prior events,≥3prior observations; need≥12supported races,60half pairs,60temporal rows. Signature gate requires halfcorr≥.30and lower95%>0, temporalcorr≥.15, positive MSE gain versus0and seed204within-event shuffled-history null with both lower95%>0. Fixed4000race-cluster bootstrap seed191. Separate unchanged E211utility protocol uses only2019frozen E194future pace-error labels, team means and race centering;≥60rows/12events; expected positive prior-duration/later-error correlation≥.15with lower95%>0. Fullpass=signature AND utility. Failure closes exact source/band/filter/team-history/utility formulation; no alternate band, throttle, sign, circuit/entity/model/decay search. Pass only permits separately registered inner winner-feature test, not goal success.

**Contract and implementation:** new acceleration(car,origin) returns COLS-shaped segment frame, empty when unsupported; source_segments only called by new main, existing shared functions unchanged. Main propagates cache failures, no retries/fallback endpoints. Numeric raw units/boolean Brake and raw-vs-merged calculation documented in official FastF1source/current Context7 docs (github.com/theoehrly/fast-f1 docs/data_reference/howto_accurate_calculations.rst; _api.car_data docstring). Synthetic known1second interpolation, empty/one-row, padding, invalid throttle/brake/speed, nonmonotonicity, gaps, duplicate dates, DRS independence pass. Rigorous-coding/ponytail applied: reuse aggregation/strictpast/metrics, no production abstraction/dependency. Require independent per-lap raw reconstruction, team/past/null/bootstrap/utility audit and actual current/future source mutation versus earlier responsiveness; standard five guards. No new winner feature or accuracy scoring yet; winner paired bootstrap/null not applicable to source-only study. Commit/push setup before measurement and results separately.

**E232 execution bug / reporting repair:** setup422aefb pushed before offline extraction. All21source passes completed with zero accepted segments. The reused E211aggregator then raised TypeError because the new empty duration column had object dtype. A synthetic empty-transit→team-table regression reproduces the failure; new extractor now explicitly types duration float for both empty-return paths, and the same regression passes. Shared E211code and every measurement/gate constant unchanged. An artifact audit was launched before the summary existed and stopped with FileNotFoundError; it did not assess a result. Rerun the offline extraction after this reporting-only repair, with no new acquisition or modified eligibility. Source audit must independently confirm the zero counts; no threshold relaxation or performance interpretation from absent observations.

**Result (E232): source coverage gate FAILS.** Reporting repair b626770 pushed before the second offline run; no new request or extraction-rule change. Both extractions report **zero accepted transits in each of all21races**. All210listed team/event rows remain, with missing response/halves, zero prior counts and zero prior predictions. Supported races, half pairs, temporal targets and utility rows are all0. Signature/utility/joint gates false. No correlations, MSE improvements or bootstrap/null performance estimates are defined; do not describe absent measurements as zero acceleration or no physical acceleration effect.

Independent audit reloaded all21raw cached feeds offline/HTTPblocked, reconstructed clean-lap masks and per-lap sample arrays, located100crossings then independently searched150crossings/interpolated and checked all channel/gap/monotonicity/count/duration rules. It reproduces zero accepted segments in every event. **Result (E241): the registered immediate-interval interpretation fails.** Setup `7a45a01` was pushed before association measurement. All three pilots pass the count requirements, but neither orientation reaches 95% agreement in any pilot, and the preferred orientation changes between races.

| 2019 pilot | Emitted numeric codes | Scored pairs | Code 1: decrease / increase | Code 2: decrease / increase | Mapping A agreement | Mapping B agreement |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| Spain R5 | 661 | 565 | 159 / 95 | 166 / 145 | 53.81% | 46.19% |
| Hungary R12 | 306 | 248 | 55 / 50 | 109 / 34 | 35.89% | 64.11% |
| Italy R14 | 381 | 315 | 64 / 70 | 143 / 38 | 32.38% | 67.62% |

There are 1,348 emitted numeric code updates and 1,128 scored pairs. The 220 unscored updates comprise 191 missing pairs and 29 stale/backward-clock pairs. Mapping A means code 1 accompanies a decreasing numeric Interval and code 2 an increasing one; mapping B reverses those labels. Spain favours A, Hungary and Italy B. Qualified pilots are A: 0, B: 0; the joint gate fails and no mapping is admitted. These percentages concern field interpretation, not race-winner accuracy.

**Verification:** an independent audit builds indexed position and Interval timelines and uses binary search for current transactional positions and strictly earlier Interval observations. It reconstructs every emitted event, opponent identity, rejection, sign, contingency cell, both orientation agreements, support flags, consistency decision and compressed input hash. Synthetic boundary, missing-value, changed-opponent, transaction, empty and source-identity checks pass. No source, threshold, lag, orientation-per-race, history or model was changed after measurement. No raw body was reacquired, no forecast feature was created and no winner model was fitted; standard five guards last passed E235 because the forecast path remains unchanged.

**Verdict:** do not interpret this field as an immediate same-opponent gap-direction flag under the registered numeric projection and 10-second pairing. Its official meaning and formula remain unresolved. This result does not prove that Catching has no predictive information or identify an alternative meaning. Do not search alternative gaps, lags, windows, signs, periods or source encodings to rescue this test without genuinely independent semantic evidence. No inner/outer/2026 performance evaluation or accuracy change. Saved `results/e241_catching_direction.json` and `tests/test_catching_direction{,_artifact}.py`. The 75% exact pre-weekend winner goal remains active and unmet.

**Next avenue to qualify, not a result:** a repository search found no aerodynamic-testing resource-allocation (ATR/wind-tunnel/CFD) experiment. A lagged, ordinary resource-entitlement proxy derived from existing structured constructor standings might supply a forward development mechanism absent from telemetry summaries. General rule timing and the distinction between entitlement, actual use and penalties need verification before any exposure or outcome calculation. Per-event upgrade/budget-penalty documents remain excluded; no new feature, regulatory coefficient or gain has been admitted here.

### E242 — Historical official constructor-standing snapshots (registered before acquisition)

The previous turn made progress: E241's failed Catching interpretation was independently audited, logged and pushed as `1c07b6e`. No acquisition process is running. Repository searches found no prior ordinary aerodynamic-testing resource-allocation experiment. Before defining a development-opportunity proxy, verify its possible structured standings source. The current `t_season_pts` feature sums race-result points; official constructor standings can differ through sprint points, championship adjustments and countback. This source audit does not test a resource effect or reopen a closed feature/model.

**Scope and eligibility:** use the existing public Jolpica structured-data service, not FIA/Pirelli articles, allocation tables, penalty notices or document-extracted event facts. Fetch exactly three historical constructor-standing tables: 2020 R17 (reference 2021-01-01), 2021 R8 (reference 2021-07-01), and 2021 R22 (reference 2022-01-01). The final-2020 pilot deliberately includes a known constructor-points-adjustment setting to test whether race sums can be substituted; this is a source diagnostic, not an unbiased performance sample. References are proposed study boundaries, not verified regulatory allocation dates or publication timestamps. No resource percentage, legal entitlement, actual testing use, penalty feature or forecast exposure is computed.

Current primary Jolpica endpoint documentation, retrieved through Context7, specifies an explicit season/round constructor-standings route, `MRData.StandingsTable.StandingsLists[].ConstructorStandings` and shared limit/offset parameters: https://github.com/jolpica/jolpica-f1/blob/main/docs/endpoints/constructorStandings.md . Documentation defines the API only and supplies no model feature values. Use the documented lowercase trailing-slash path with Accept: application/json, limit=100, offset=0. No `current`/`last` routes or 2026 query. The service is already the project's public results source; no account, payment, licence acceptance or private credentials are needed.

**Bounded acquisition:** three application GETs at most, one per exact year/round/parameter URL, through the existing persistent HTTP cache. Record cache status and actual allowlisted network sends; a cache hit need not send anything. Timeout 20 seconds, redirects disabled, no alternate endpoint, pagination or automatic/manual retry. Sleep 0.4 seconds after an uncached response as the existing data layer does. Accept response bodies no larger than 1 MiB after download; this is an acceptance limit, not a streaming-memory guarantee. Checkpoint each attempted snapshot, including failures, and resume only unattempted keys. Preserve accepted raw public JSON as deterministic gzip plus SHA256 so its projection can be independently replayed. Raw API name/nationality/URL fields are not model inputs; the projection reads only constructor ID, rank, points and wins.

**Schema and source gate:** require HTTP200, exactly one matching season/round standings list and matching table tags, offset0/total10, exactly ten unique constructor IDs and ranks1..10. Points must be finite/nonnegative; rank/wins integer/nonnegative, wins no greater than the requested round. Reject booleans as numbers and invalid IDs. IDs must exactly match local participants through that historical round. All source-event dates must precede their stated references. Overall gate: all three attempts completed and at least two qualifying snapshots. This verifies source format/identity/calendar order only. Current historical API tables may incorporate later corrections; an event date or retrieval header does not prove what was published by an original cutoff. Historical publication, actual allocation rules/use and feature admission remain explicitly unverified even after a pass.

**Fixed diagnostic:** on each accepted table, compare official points with local race-only sums and race-plus-sprint sums for the same year/round prefix. Save both differences and strict ordering inversions between official ranks and combined-point sums; tied sums do not count as inversions. Do not derive penalty causes from a difference, adjust local labels, replace baselines or fit a predictor. Source failure closes this exact snapshot/API/schema protocol without a route/year/sample change to force coverage. A pass permits separately registered source-timing/identity/exposure work, not a direct winner test or a 75% claim.

**Verification:** deliberate-thinking, rigorous-coding, ponytail and git-workflow applied. Traced existing HTTP cache, allowlist and point-feature code before adding new callers; shared modules remain unchanged. Synthetic season/round/pagination/duplicate/rank/NaN/boolean/win-bound rejection, ignored poisoned text fields, known point adjustments and strict ordering-inversion checks pass. Require independent decompressed raw-body/schema projection, metadata/hash/request-scope/calendar checks and scalar prefix point/sprint/inversion reconstruction. No forecast feature changes; five standard guards last passed E235, with fresh source-mutation/guards required before any later feature. No winner/podium accuracy, paired winner bootstrap, null fit or outer/2026 evaluation applies to this source audit. Commit/push setup before acquisition and results separately. The 75% exact pre-weekend winner goal remains active and unmet.

**Result (E242): source format/identity/calendar gate passes 3/3.** Setup `413ecbd` was pushed before three exact historical API requests. All returned HTTP200 without cache hits, redirects, retries or alternate endpoints; accepted raw bodies total 6,931 bytes. Every table has ten unique constructor IDs/ranks and matches the requested year/round, local participants and source-event-before-reference constraint. Raw JSON is preserved, so its projection can be replayed independently. This pass does not verify historical publication timing, resource entitlement, actual usage or predictive value.

The 2020 final table reports Racing Point P4 with **195 points**, while local race-only and race-plus-sprint sums both give **210**. The resulting strict ordering inversion is McLaren/Racing Point: a raw point sum puts Racing Point above McLaren, unlike the championship table. The numerical difference is **−15**; no penalty notice was ingested and no cause is inferred from arithmetic alone. This confirms that an actual standings-dependent study cannot silently substitute race-point sums for published constructor positions.

The 2021 R8 table agrees with both local sums. In the 2021 final table, race-only sums are lower than official totals by Mercedes **9**, Red Bull **7**, Ferrari **1**, and McLaren **1**. Adding the existing sprint-result points reconciles all ten teams exactly. Neither 2021 snapshot has a strict combined-point ordering inversion. These are source diagnostics on purposively selected snapshots, not performance estimates or a reason to alter frozen model features with their existing race-points definition.

**Independent verification:** decompressed raw response bytes reproduce every constructor ID/rank/point/win, envelope/pagination tag, SHA256, byte bound, deterministic gzip header, exact URL/status/cache/send count and calendar check. A separate scalar accumulation over the local historical prefixes reproduces all race/sprint sums, differences and ordering inversions without calling the new projector/comparison helpers. All source-schema, ignored poisoned text, numeric/identity and point-adjustment synthetics pass. No exposure or predictor was computed, no document-derived feature entered, no existing data labels/production feature/baseline were changed, and no outer/2026 evaluation occurred. Five forecast guards last passed E235; future feature work needs new source-mutation/guard checks.

**Verdict / next:** a distinct, structured constructor-standing source is available and its distinction from race-only points is verified. Define and register a lagged standings-derived development-opportunity proxy, its family mapping and temporal availability assumptions before measuring any association with future pace or winners. Do not call it actual testing allocation/use, use per-event allocation/penalty documents, assume an event date proves publication, or treat three available tables as coverage of an entire forecast experiment. Saved `results/e242_constructor_snapshots.json`, three `e242_*_raw.json.gz` responses and `tests/test_constructor_snapshots{,_artifact}.py`. No accuracy gain is claimed; the 75% exact pre-weekend winner goal remains active and unmet.
